# Bitácora de trabajo con Codex

Este documento registra cronológicamente las consultas y órdenes del usuario, junto con las respuestas, comentarios, decisiones y acciones relevantes de Codex en este directorio.

# Repository Guidelines

## Project Structure & Module Organization

This repository is a small financial dashboard split into two services. `backend/app/` contains the FastAPI application, SQLAlchemy models, spreadsheet import logic, categorization rules, and analytics. Backend tests live in `backend/tests/`. `frontend/src/` contains the React UI; `main.jsx` holds the views and `api/client.js` centralizes API calls. Static styling is in `frontend/src/styles.css`. Docker definitions live in `backend/Dockerfile`, `frontend/Dockerfile`, and the root `docker-compose.yml`. Root-level `.xls`, `.xlsx`, and `.ods` files are data inputs, while `backend/finance.db` is the local SQLite store.

## Build, Test, and Development Commands

- `docker compose up --build`: build both services and serve the UI at `http://localhost:8080`.
- `cd backend && python -m venv .venv && .venv/bin/pip install -r requirements.txt`: create a backend environment and install dependencies.
- `cd backend && .venv/bin/uvicorn app.main:app --reload`: run the API at `http://127.0.0.1:8000`.
- `cd backend && .venv/bin/python -m pytest`: run the backend test suite.
- `cd frontend && npm ci && npm run dev`: install pinned frontend dependencies and start Vite at `http://127.0.0.1:5173`.
- `cd frontend && npm run build`: produce the production bundle in `frontend/dist/`.

## Coding Style & Naming Conventions

Follow existing patterns: four-space indentation and `snake_case` for Python functions and modules; two-space indentation, `camelCase` variables, and `PascalCase` React components in JavaScript. Keep API paths under `/api`, add type hints to backend functions, and keep database access scoped to injected SQLAlchemy sessions. No formatter or linter is configured, so keep imports grouped and changes consistent with adjacent code.

## Testing Guidelines

Tests use pytest and follow `test_*.py` / `test_*` naming. Add focused tests near the affected module, using in-memory SQLite and generated workbook fixtures where possible. Run the full backend suite and a frontend production build before submitting. There is no enforced coverage threshold; cover regressions and important import edge cases.

## Commit & Pull Request Guidelines

No usable Git history is present in this checkout. Use short, imperative, scoped commits, for example `Fix duplicate transaction grouping`. Pull requests should explain behavior changes, list validation commands, link relevant issues, and include screenshots for UI changes.

## Security & Data Handling

Treat bank exports, `finance.db`, backups, and uploaded observations as sensitive. Do not add new real financial data or credentials to commits. Use synthetic, minimal fixtures in tests and review Docker volume changes carefully.

--- compose ---
services:
  backend:
    build:
      context: .
      dockerfile: backend/Dockerfile
    container_name: bancosia-backend
    restart: unless-stopped
    environment:
      BANCOSIA_USERNAME: ${BANCOSIA_USERNAME:?Define BANCOSIA_USERNAME en el archivo .env}
      BANCOSIA_PASSWORD: ${BANCOSIA_PASSWORD:?Define BANCOSIA_PASSWORD en el archivo .env}
      BANCOSIA_SESSION_SECRET: ${BANCOSIA_SESSION_SECRET:?Define BANCOSIA_SESSION_SECRET en el archivo .env}
      BANCOSIA_SESSION_HOURS: ${BANCOSIA_SESSION_HOURS:-12}
      BANCOSIA_COOKIE_SECURE: ${BANCOSIA_COOKIE_SECURE:-false}
    volumes:
      - ./backend/finance.db:/workspace/backend/finance.db
      - ./backend/uploads:/workspace/backend/uploads
      - ./TotalBancosIA_paraApp.xlsx:/workspace/TotalBancosIA_paraApp.xlsx
    expose:
      - "8000"

  frontend:
    build:
      context: .

    }
}

--- auth ---
import base64
import hashlib
import hmac
import json
import os
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass

from fastapi import Request


SESSION_COOKIE_NAME = "bancosia_session"
DEFAULT_SESSION_HOURS = 12


class AuthConfigurationError(RuntimeError):
    pass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AuthSettings:
    username: str
    password: str
    session_secret: str
    session_seconds: int
    cookie_secure: bool


def get_auth_settings() -> AuthSettings:
    username = os.getenv("BANCOSIA_USERNAME", "").strip()
    password = os.getenv("BANCOSIA_PASSWORD", "")
    session_secret = os.getenv("BANCOSIA_SESSION_SECRET", "")
    if not username or not password:
        raise AuthConfigurationError("BANCOSIA_USERNAME y BANCOSIA_PASSWORD son obligatorios")
    if len(session_secret) < 32:
        raise AuthConfigurationError("BANCOSIA_SESSION_SECRET debe tener al menos 32 caracteres")

    try:
        session_hours = int(os.getenv("BANCOSIA_SESSION_HOURS", str(DEFAULT_SESSION_HOURS)))
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise AuthConfigurationError("BANCOSIA_SESSION_HOURS debe ser un numero entero") from exc
    if session_hours < 1:
        raise AuthConfigurationError("BANCOSIA_SESSION_HOURS debe ser mayor que cero")

    cookie_secure = os.getenv("BANCOSIA_COOKIE_SECURE", "false").strip().lower() in {"1", "true", "yes", "on"}
    return AuthSettings(username, password, session_secret, session_hours * 3600, cookie_secure)


def credentials_are_valid(username: str, password: str, settings: AuthSettings) -> bool:
    return hmac.compare_digest(username.encode(), settings.username.encode()) and hmac.compare_digest(
        password.encode(), settings.password.encode()
    )


def create_session_token(username: str, settings: AuthSettings, now: int | None = None) -> str:
    payload = {
        "sub": username,
        "exp": (now if now is not None else int(time.time())) + settings.session_seconds,
    }
    encoded_payload = _encode(json.dumps(payload, separators=(",", ":")).encode())
    signature = hmac.new(settings.session_secret.encode(), encoded_payload.encode(), hashlib.sha256).digest()
    return f"{encoded_payload}.{_encode(signature)}"


def session_username(token: str | None, settings: AuthSettings, now: int | None = None) -> str | None:
    if not token:
        return None
    try:
        encoded_payload, encoded_signature = token.split(".", 1)
        expected_signature = hmac.new(
            settings.session_secret.encode(), encoded_payload.encode(), hashlib.sha256
        ).digest()
        if not hmac.compare_digest(_decode(encoded_signature), expected_signature):
            return None
        payload = json.loads(_decode(encoded_payload))
        current_time = now if now is not None else int(time.time())
        if payload.get("sub") != settings.username or int(payload.get("exp", 0)) <= current_time:
            return None
        return settings.username
    except (ValueError, TypeError, json.JSONDecodeError):
        return None


def authenticated_username(request: Request, settings: AuthSettings) -> str | None:
    return session_username(request.cookies.get(SESSION_COOKIE_NAME), settings)


def _encode(value: bytes) -> str:
    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(value).rstrip(b"=").decode()


def _decode(value: str) -> bytes:
    return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(value + "=" * (-len(value) % 4))

PUBLIC_API_PATHS = {"/api/health", "/api/auth/login", "/api/auth/session", "/api/auth/logout"}


@app.middleware("http")
async def require_authentication(request: Request, call_next):
    if request.method == "OPTIONS" or not request.url.path.startswith("/api/") or request.url.path in PUBLIC_API_PATHS:
        return await call_next(request)
    try:
        settings = get_auth_settings()
    except AuthConfigurationError as exc:
        return JSONResponse(status_code=503, content={"detail": str(exc)})
    if not authenticated_username(request, settings):
        return JSONResponse(status_code=401, content={"detail": "Debes iniciar sesion"})
    return await call_next(request)


@app.on_event("startup")
def on_startup() -> None:
    init_db()


@app.get("/api/health")
def health() -> dict[str, str]:
    return {"status": "ok"}


@app.post("/api/auth/login")
def login(payload: LoginIn, response: Response) -> dict[str, str | bool]:
    try:
        settings = get_auth_settings()
    except AuthConfigurationError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail=str(exc)) from exc
    if not credentials_are_valid(payload.username, payload.password, settings):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Usuario o contrasena incorrectos")
    response.set_cookie(
        key=SESSION_COOKIE_NAME,
        value=create_session_token(settings.username, settings),
        max_age=settings.session_seconds,
        httponly=True,
        secure=settings.cookie_secure,
        samesite="strict",
        path="/api",
    )
    response.headers["Cache-Control"] = "no-store"
    return {"authenticated": True, "username": settings.username}


@app.get("/api/auth/session")
def auth_session(request: Request) -> dict[str, str | bool | None]:
    try:
        settings = get_auth_settings()
    except AuthConfigurationError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail=str(exc)) from exc
    username = authenticated_username(request, settings)
    return {"authenticated": bool(username), "username": username}


@app.post("/api/auth/logout")
def logout(response: Response) -> dict[str, bool]:
    response.delete_cookie(SESSION_COOKIE_NAME, path="/api")
    response.headers["Cache-Control"] = "no-store"
    return {"authenticated": False}


def _normalize_sql(query: str) -> str:
    return query.strip().rstrip(";").strip()


def _validate_readonly_sql(query: str) -> str:
    normalized = _normalize_sql(query)
    lowered = normalized.lower()
    forbidden = {
        "insert",
        "update",
        "delete",
        "drop",
        "alter",
        "create",
        "replace",
        "truncate",
        "attach",
        "detach",
        "pragma",
        "vacuum",
        "reindex",
    }
    if not normalized:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="La consulta esta vacia")
    if ";" in normalized:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Solo se permite una sentencia SQL")
    if "--" in normalized or "/*" in normalized or "*/" in normalized:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="No se permiten comentarios SQL")
    first_word = lowered.split(None, 1)[0]
    if first_word not in {"select", "with"}:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Solo se permiten consultas SELECT")
    words = {word.strip("(),") for word in lowered.replace("\n", " ").split()}
    if words & forbidden:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="La consulta contiene una palabra no permitida")
    return normalized


@app.get("/api/sql/schema")
def sql_schema() -> dict:
    inspector = inspect(engine)
    tables = []
    for table_name in inspector.get_table_names():
        columns = inspector.get_columns(table_name)
        tables.append(
            {
                "name": table_name,
                "columns": [
                    {
                        "name": column["name"],
                        "type": str(column["type"]),
                        "nullable": bool(column.get("nullable", True)),
                        "primary_key": bool(column.get("primary_key", False)),
                    }
                    for column in columns
                ],
            }
        )
    return {"tables": tables}


@app.post("/api/sql/query")
def sql_query(payload: SqlQueryIn, db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict:
    query = _validate_readonly_sql(payload.query)
    limit = max(1, min(payload.limit or 200, 1000))
    try:
        result = db.execute(text(f"SELECT * FROM ({query}) AS readonly_query LIMIT :limit"), {"limit": limit})
    except Exception as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(exc)) from exc
    rows = [dict(row._mapping) for row in result.fetchall()]
    columns = list(result.keys())
    return {"columns": columns, "rows": rows, "row_count": len(rows), "limit": limit}

--- auth tests ---
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app.auth import create_session_token, get_auth_settings, session_username
from app.main import app


def configure_auth(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("BANCOSIA_USERNAME", "usuario-prueba")
    monkeypatch.setenv("BANCOSIA_PASSWORD", "contrasena-prueba")
    monkeypatch.setenv("BANCOSIA_SESSION_SECRET", "secreto-de-prueba-con-mas-de-32-caracteres")


def test_protected_api_requires_login(monkeypatch):
    configure_auth(monkeypatch)
    client = TestClient(app)

    response = client.get("/api/entities")

    assert response.status_code == 401


def test_login_creates_session_and_logout_removes_it(monkeypatch):
    configure_auth(monkeypatch)
    client = TestClient(app)

    bad_login = client.post("/api/auth/login", json={"username": "usuario-prueba", "password": "incorrecta"})
    assert bad_login.status_code == 401

    login = client.post(
        "/api/auth/login",
        json={"username": "usuario-prueba", "password": "contrasena-prueba"},
    )
    assert login.status_code == 200
    assert client.get("/api/auth/session").json() == {
        "authenticated": True,
        "username": "usuario-prueba",
    }

    logout = client.post("/api/auth/logout")
    assert logout.status_code == 200
    assert client.get("/api/auth/session").json()["authenticated"] is False


def test_expired_and_modified_tokens_are_rejected(monkeypatch):
    configure_auth(monkeypatch)
    settings = get_auth_settings()
    token = create_session_token(settings.username, settings, now=100)

    assert session_username(token, settings, now=101) == settings.username
    assert session_username(token, settings, now=100 + settings.session_seconds) is None
    assert session_username(f"{token}modificado", settings, now=101) is None

Assistant

La plantilla usa dos contenedores: FastAPI solo accesible dentro de la red de Compose y Nginx como único puerto público; la autenticación se basa en una cookie de sesión firmada y variables secretas en .env. Ahora estoy adaptando esa misma protección al API y a la interfaz de CowsIA, además de identificar los directorios de imágenes/datos que deben persistir mediante volúmenes.

@app.get("/api/animals", response_model=list[AnimalOut])
def list_animals(
    q: str | None = None,
    status: str | None = None,
    sexo: list[str] = Query(default_factory=list),
    raza: str | None = None,
    causa_baja: str | None = None,
    explotacion: str | None = None,
    born_from: date | None = None,
    born_to: date | None = None,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
) -> list[Animal]:
    query = db.query(Animal)
    if q:
        pattern = f"%{q}%"
        query = query.filter(or_(Animal.crotal.ilike(pattern), Animal.crotal_madre.ilike(pattern), Animal.procedencia.ilike(pattern), Animal.destino.ilike(pattern)))
    if status == "Activo":
        query = query.filter(Animal.fecha_baja.is_(None), or_(Animal.causa_baja.is_(None), Animal.causa_baja == ""))
    if status == "Baja":
        query = query.filter(or_(Animal.fecha_baja.is_not(None), Animal.causa_baja.is_not(None) & (Animal.causa_baja != "")))
    if sexo:
        query = query.filter(Animal.sexo.in_(sexo))
    if raza:
        query = query.filter(Animal.raza == raza)
    if causa_baja:
        query = query.filter(Animal.causa_baja == causa_baja)
    if explotacion:
        query = query.filter(or_(Animal.explotacion_pertenencia == explotacion, Animal.explotacion_ubicacion == explotacion, Animal.explotacion_nacimiento == explotacion))
    if born_from:
        query = query.filter(Animal.fecha_nacimiento >= born_from)
    if born_to:
        query = query.filter(Animal.fecha_nacimiento <= born_to)
    return query.order_by(Animal.fecha_nacimiento.desc().nullslast(), Animal.crotal.asc()).all()


def _animal_by_crotal(db: Session, crotal: str) -> Animal:
    animal = db.query(Animal).filter(Animal.crotal == crotal).first()
    if not animal:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Animal no encontrado")
    return animal


def _image_suffix(file: UploadFile) -> str:
    suffix = ALLOWED_IMAGE_TYPES.get(file.content_type or "")
    if not suffix:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="Solo se admiten imagenes JPG, PNG, WEBP o GIF")
    return suffix


def _store_upload(folder: str, file: UploadFile, content: bytes, suffix: str) -> Path:
    target_dir = UPLOAD_ROOT / folder
    target_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    target = target_dir / f"{uuid4().hex}{suffix}"
    target.write_bytes(content)
    return target


def _image_out(row: AnimalImage) -> dict:
    return {
        "id": row.id,
        "animal_id": row.animal_id,
        "file_name": row.file_name,
        "content_type": row.content_type,
        "is_primary": bool(row.is_primary),
        "created_at": row.created_at,
        "image_url": f"/api/animal-images/{row.id}/file",
    }


def _event_out(row: AnimalEvent) -> dict:
    return {
        "id": row.id,
        "animal_id": row.animal_id,
        "text": row.text,
        "event_date": row.event_date,
        "reporter": row.reporter,
        "image_name": row.image_name,
        "created_at": row.created_at,
        "image_url": f"/api/animal-events/{row.id}/image" if row.image_path else None,
    }


def _age_years(born: date | None) -> float | None:
    if not born:
        return None
    return round((date.today() - born).days / 365.25, 1)


@app.get("/api/animals/{crotal}/details", response_model=AnimalDetailOut)
def animal_details(crotal: str, db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict:
    animal = _animal_by_crotal(db, crotal)
    images = db.query(AnimalImage).filter(AnimalImage.animal_id == animal.id).order_by(AnimalImage.is_primary.desc(), AnimalImage.created_at.asc(), AnimalImage.id.asc()).all()
    locations = db.query(AnimalLocation).filter(AnimalLocation.animal_id == animal.id).order_by(AnimalLocation.recorded_at.desc()).all()
    events = db.query(AnimalEvent).filter(AnimalEvent.animal_id == animal.id).order_by(AnimalEvent.event_date.desc().nullslast(), AnimalEvent.created_at.desc()).all()
    return {
        "animal": animal,
        "images": [_image_out(row) for row in images],
        "locations": locations,
        "events": [_event_out(row) for row in events],
    }


@app.post("/api/animals/{crotal}/images", response_model=AnimalImageOut)
async def create_animal_image(crotal: str, file: UploadFile = File(...), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict:
    animal = _animal_by_crotal(db, crotal)
    suffix = _image_suffix(file)
    content = await file.read()
    target = _store_upload("animal_images", file, content, suffix)
    row = AnimalImage(
        animal_id=animal.id,
        file_path=str(target),
        file_name=file.filename or target.name,
        content_type=file.content_type,
        is_primary=db.query(AnimalImage).filter(AnimalImage.animal_id == animal.id, AnimalImage.is_primary.is_(True)).first() is None,
    )
    db.add(row)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(row)
    return _image_out(row)


@app.get("/api/gallery", response_model=list[GalleryAnimalOut])
def gallery(db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> list[dict]:
    animals = db.query(Animal).order_by(Animal.crotal.asc()).all()
    rows = []
    for animal in animals:
        image = (
            db.query(AnimalImage)
            .filter(AnimalImage.animal_id == animal.id)
            .order_by(AnimalImage.is_primary.desc(), AnimalImage.created_at.asc(), AnimalImage.id.asc())
            .first()
        )
        rows.append(
            {
                "id": animal.id,
                "crotal": animal.crotal,
                "sexo": animal.sexo,
                "fecha_nacimiento": animal.fecha_nacimiento,
                "age_years": _age_years(animal.fecha_nacimiento),
                "image_id": image.id if image else None,
                "image_url": f"/api/animal-images/{image.id}/file" if image else None,
                "file_name": image.file_name if image else None,
            }
        )
    return rows


@app.get("/api/map/locations", response_model=list[MapLocationOut])
def map_locations(active_only: bool = True, db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> list[dict]:
    query = db.query(Animal)
    if active_only:
        query = query.filter(Animal.fecha_baja.is_(None), or_(Animal.causa_baja.is_(None), Animal.causa_baja == ""))
    animals = query.order_by(Animal.crotal.asc()).all()
    rows = []
    for animal in animals:
        location = (
            db.query(AnimalLocation)
            .filter(AnimalLocation.animal_id == animal.id)
            .order_by(AnimalLocation.recorded_at.desc(), AnimalLocation.id.desc())
            .first()
        )
        if not location:
            continue
        image = (
            db.query(AnimalImage)
            .filter(AnimalImage.animal_id == animal.id)
            .order_by(AnimalImage.is_primary.desc(), AnimalImage.created_at.asc(), AnimalImage.id.asc())
            .first()
        )
        rows.append(
            {
                "animal_id": animal.id,
                "crotal": animal.crotal,
                "sexo": animal.sexo,
                "fecha_nacimiento": animal.fecha_nacimiento,
                "age_years": _age_years(animal.fecha_nacimiento),
                "latitude": location.latitude,
                "longitude": location.longitude,
                "recorded_at": location.recorded_at,
                "source": location.source,
                "is_active": not (animal.fecha_baja or animal.causa_baja),
                "image_url": f"/api/animal-images/{image.id}/file" if image else None,
            }
        )
    return rows


@app.post("/api/animal-images/{image_id}/primary", response_model=AnimalImageOut)
def set_primary_animal_image(image_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict:
    row = db.get(AnimalImage, image_id)
    if not row:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Imagen no encontrada")
    for image in db.query(AnimalImage).filter(AnimalImage.animal_id == row.animal_id).all():
        image.is_primary = image.id == row.id
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(row)
    return _image_out(row)


@app.get("/api/animal-images/{image_id}/file")
def get_animal_image(image_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> FileResponse:
    row = db.get(AnimalImage, image_id)
    if not row:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Imagen no encontrada")
    path = Path(row.file_path)
    if not path.exists():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Imagen no encontrada en disco")
    return FileResponse(path, filename=row.file_name or path.name, media_type=row.content_type)


@app.delete("/api/animal-images/{image_id}")
def delete_animal_image(image_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict[str, bool]:
    row = db.get(AnimalImage, image_id)
    if not row:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Imagen no encontrada")
    path = Path(row.file_path)
    if path.exists():
        path.unlink()
    animal_id = row.animal_id
    was_primary = bool(row.is_primary)
    db.delete(row)
    db.flush()
    if was_primary:
        next_image = (
            db.query(AnimalImage)
            .filter(AnimalImage.animal_id == animal_id)
            .order_by(AnimalImage.created_at.asc(), AnimalImage.id.asc())
            .first()
        )
        if next_image:
            next_image.is_primary = True
    db.commit()
    return {"ok": True}


@app.post("/api/animals/{crotal}/locations", response_model=AnimalLocationOut)
def create_animal_location(crotal: str, payload: AnimalLocationIn, db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> AnimalLocation:
    animal = _animal_by_crotal(db, crotal)
    row = AnimalLocation(
        animal_id=animal.id,
        latitude=payload.latitude,
        longitude=payload.longitude,
        recorded_at=payload.recorded_at or datetime.utcnow(),
        source=payload.source,
    )
    db.add(row)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(row)
    return row


@app.delete("/api/animal-locations/{location_id}")
def delete_animal_location(location_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict[str, bool]:
    row = db.get(AnimalLocation, location_id)
    if not row:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Ubicacion no encontrada")
    db.delete(row)
    db.commit()
    return {"ok": True}


@app.post("/api/animals/{crotal}/events", response_model=AnimalEventOut)
def create_animal_event(crotal: str, payload: AnimalEventIn, db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict:
    animal = _animal_by_crotal(db, crotal)
    text = payload.text.strip()
    if not text:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="El texto del suceso no puede estar vacio")
    row = AnimalEvent(animal_id=animal.id, text=text, event_date=payload.event_date, reporter=payload.reporter)
    db.add(row)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(row)
    return _event_out(row)

async def create_animal_event_with_image(
    crotal: str,
    text: str = Form(...),

from datetime import date, datetime

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict


class AnimalOut(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)

    id: int
    crotal: str
    fecha_inicio: date | None
    fecha_fin: date | None
    explotacion_pertenencia: str | None
    explotacion_ubicacion: str | None
    fecha_nacimiento: date | None
    explotacion_nacimiento: str | None
    sexo: str | None
    raza: str | None
    crotal_madre: str | None
    causa_alta: str | None
    fecha_alta: date | None
    procedencia: str | None
    codigo_local_entrada: str | None
    codigo_remo_entrada: str | None
    causa_baja: str | None
    fecha_baja: date | None
    destino: str | None
    codigo_local_salida: str | None
    codigo_remo_salida: str | None
    source_file: str | None

--- Cows database ---
from collections.abc import Generator

from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy import inspect, text
from sqlalchemy.orm import DeclarativeBase, Session, sessionmaker

DATABASE_URL = "sqlite:///./cows.db"

engine = create_engine(DATABASE_URL, connect_args={"check_same_thread": False})
SessionLocal = sessionmaker(bind=engine, autoflush=False, autocommit=False)


class Base(DeclarativeBase):
    pass


def get_db() -> Generator[Session, None, None]:
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        yield db
    finally:
        db.close()


def init_db() -> None:
    from app import models  # noqa: F401

    Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)
    ensure_sqlite_columns()
    ensure_primary_animal_images()


def ensure_sqlite_columns() -> None:
    inspector = inspect(engine)
    tables = inspector.get_table_names()
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        if "animal_images" in tables:
            columns = {column["name"] for column in inspector.get_columns("animal_images")}
            if "is_primary" not in columns:
                connection.execute(text("ALTER TABLE animal_images ADD COLUMN is_primary BOOLEAN DEFAULT 0"))


def ensure_primary_animal_images() -> None:
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        tables = inspect(engine).get_table_names()
        if "animal_images" not in tables:
            return
        animal_ids = [
            row[0]
            for row in connection.execute(text("SELECT DISTINCT animal_id FROM animal_images")).fetchall()
        ]
        for animal_id in animal_ids:
            primary_count = connection.execute(
                text("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM animal_images WHERE animal_id = :animal_id AND is_primary = 1"),
                {"animal_id": animal_id},
            ).scalar_one()
            if primary_count:
                continue
            first_image_id = connection.execute(
                text("SELECT id FROM animal_images WHERE animal_id = :animal_id ORDER BY created_at ASC, id ASC LIMIT 1"),
                {"animal_id": animal_id},
            ).scalar_one_or_none()
            if first_image_id is not None:
                connection.execute(
                    text("UPDATE animal_images SET is_primary = 1 WHERE id = :image_id"),
                    {"image_id": first_image_id},
                )

function formatDate(value) {
  if (!value) return "";
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-ES").format(new Date(`${value}T00:00:00`));
}

function years(value) {
  if (!value) return "";
  const born = new Date(`${value}T00:00:00`);
  const diff = (Date.now() - born.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24 * 365.25);
  return `${diff.toFixed(1)} anos`;
}

function toQueryString(values) {
  const params = new URLSearchParams();
  Object.entries(values).forEach(([key, value]) => {
    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
      value.filter(Boolean).forEach((item) => params.append(key, item));
    } else if (value) {
      params.append(key, value);
    }
  });
  const suffix = params.toString();
  return suffix ? `?${suffix}` : "";
}

function App() {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState("dashboard");
  const [refreshKey, setRefreshKey] = useState(0);

  function refresh() {
    setRefreshKey((value) => value + 1);
  }

  return (
    <div className="app-shell">
      <aside className="sidebar">
        <div className="brand">
          <Gauge size={24} />
          <div>
            <strong>CowsIA</strong>
            <span>Registro ganadero</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <nav>
          {tabs.map((tab) => {
            const Icon = tab.icon;
            return (
              <button key={tab.id} className={activeTab === tab.id ? "active" : ""} onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.id)}>
                <Icon size={18} />
                {tab.label}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </nav>
      </aside>
      <main>
        {activeTab === "dashboard" && <Dashboard refreshKey={refreshKey} />}
        {activeTab === "gallery" && <Gallery refreshKey={refreshKey} />}
        {activeTab === "map" && <CowsMap refreshKey={refreshKey} />}
        {activeTab === "animals" && <Animals refreshKey={refreshKey} />}
        {activeTab === "import" && <ImportXml onRefresh={refresh} />}
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

const animalMarkerIcon = L.divIcon({
  className: "animal-map-marker",
  html: '<span></span>',
  iconSize: [22, 22],
  iconAnchor: [11, 11],
  popupAnchor: [0, -12],
});

function CowsMap({ refreshKey }) {
  const [rows, setRows] = useState([]);
  const [selectedAnimal, setSelectedAnimal] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    api.get("/api/map/locations").then(setRows);
  }, [refreshKey]);

  const center = useMemo(() => {
    if (rows.length === 0) return [39.96, -6.48];
    const lat = rows.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.latitude, 0) / rows.length;
    const lng = rows.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.longitude, 0) / rows.length;
    return [lat, lng];
  }, [rows]);

  return (
    <section className="page map-page">
      <PageTitle title="Mapa" />
      <div className="map-summary">
        <span>{rows.length} animales con ubicacion</span>
      </div>
      <div className="map-panel">
        <MapContainer key={`${center[0]}-${center[1]}-${rows.length}`} center={center} zoom={15} scrollWheelZoom className="animal-map">
          <TileLayer
            attribution='&copy; <a href="https://www.openstreetmap.org/copyright">OpenStreetMap</a>'
            url="https://{s}.tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png"
          />
          {rows.map((row) => (
            <Marker key={`${row.animal_id}-${row.latitude}-${row.longitude}`} position={[row.latitude, row.longitude]} icon={animalMarkerIcon}>
              <Popup>
                <div className="map-popup">
                  {row.image_url && <img src={api.url(row.image_url)} alt={row.crotal} />}
                  <strong>{row.crotal}</strong>
                  <span>{row.sexo || "Sin sexo"} · {row.age_years === null ? "Sin edad" : `${row.age_years} anos`}</span>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() =>
                      setSelectedAnimal({
                        crotal: row.crotal,
                        sexo: row.sexo,
                        fecha_nacimiento: row.fecha_nacimiento,
                      })
                    }
                  >
                    Abrir ficha
                  </button>
                </div>
              </Popup>
            </Marker>
          ))}
        </MapContainer>
      </div>
      {selectedAnimal && <AnimalDetail animal={selectedAnimal} onClose={() => setSelectedAnimal(null)} onChanged={() => api.get("/api/map/locations").then(setRows)} />}
    </section>
  );
}

function Gallery({ refreshKey }) {
  const [rows, setRows] = useState([]);
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const [selectedAnimal, setSelectedAnimal] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    api.get("/api/gallery").then(setRows);
  }, [refreshKey]);

  const filteredRows = useMemo(() => {
    const term = q.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!term) return rows;
    return rows.filter((row) => row.crotal.toLowerCase().includes(term) || (row.sexo || "").toLowerCase().includes(term));
  }, [rows, q]);

  return (
    <section className="page">
      <PageTitle title="Galeria" />
      <div className="gallery-toolbar">
        <label>
          <Search size={16} />
          <input placeholder="Buscar crotal o sexo" value={q} onChange={(event) => setQ(event.target.value)} />
        </label>
        <span>{filteredRows.length} animales</span>
      </div>
      <div className="gallery-grid">
        {filteredRows.map((row) => (
          <button
            className="gallery-card"
            key={row.id}
            onClick={() => setSelectedAnimal({ crotal: row.crotal, sexo: row.sexo, fecha_nacimiento: row.fecha_nacimiento })}
          >
            {row.image_url ? (
              <img src={api.url(row.image_url)} alt={row.crotal} />
            ) : (
              <div className="gallery-placeholder"><Image size={28} /></div>
            )}
            <div>
              <strong>{row.crotal}</strong>
              <span>{row.sexo || "Sin sexo"} · {row.age_years === null ? "Sin edad" : `${row.age_years} anos`}</span>
            </div>
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      {selectedAnimal && <AnimalDetail animal={selectedAnimal} onClose={() => setSelectedAnimal(null)} onChanged={() => api.get("/api/gallery").then(setRows)} />}
    </section>
  );
}

function Dashboard({ refreshKey }) {
  const [data, setData] = useState({
    summary: {},
    status: [],
    sex: [],
    breeds: [],
    birthYears: [],
    exits: [],
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    Promise.all([
      api.get("/api/dashboard/summary"),
      api.get("/api/dashboard/status"),
      api.get("/api/dashboard/sex"),
      api.get("/api/dashboard/breeds"),
      api.get("/api/dashboard/birth-years"),
      api.get("/api/dashboard/exits"),
    ]).then(([summary, status, sex, breeds, birthYears, exits]) => {
      setData({ summary, status, sex, breeds, birthYears, exits });
    });
  }, [refreshKey]);

  return (
    <section className="page">
      <PageTitle title="Dashboard" action={<ExportButtons />} />
      <div className="metrics">
        <Metric label="Animales" value={data.summary.total || 0} />
        <Metric label="Activos" value={data.summary.active || 0} tone="good" />
        <Metric label="Bajas" value={data.summary.inactive || 0} tone="bad" />
        <Metric label="Edad media activos" value={`${data.summary.average_age || 0} anos`} />
      </div>
      <div className="grid two">
        <ChartPanel title="Nacimientos por ano">
          <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={280}>
            <BarChart data={data.birthYears}>
              <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" />
              <XAxis dataKey="year" />
              <YAxis allowDecimals={false} />
              <Tooltip />
              <Bar dataKey="animals" fill="#1677ff" name="Animales" />
            </BarChart>
          </ResponsiveContainer>
        </ChartPanel>
        <ChartPanel title="Bajas por causa">
          <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={280}>
            <BarChart data={data.exits} layout="vertical" margin={{ left: 24, right: 10 }}>
              <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" />
              <XAxis type="number" allowDecimals={false} />
              <YAxis type="category" dataKey="cause" width={120} />
              <Tooltip />
              <Bar dataKey="animals" fill="#d64550" name="Animales" />
            </BarChart>
          </ResponsiveContainer>
        </ChartPanel>
      </div>
      <div className="grid three">
        <PiePanel title="Estado" rows={data.status} />
        <PiePanel title="Sexo" rows={data.sex} />
        <ListPanel title="Razas" rows={data.breeds} />
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function Animals({ refreshKey }) {
  const [rows, setRows] = useState([]);
  const [selectedAnimal, setSelectedAnimal] = useState(null);

function money(value) {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat("es-ES", { style: "currency", currency: "EUR" }).format(value || 0);

function AuthenticatedApp() {
  const [session, setSession] = useState({ loading: true, username: null });

  useEffect(() => {
    api.session()
      .then((result) => setSession({ loading: false, username: result.authenticated ? result.username : null }))
      .catch((error) => setSession({ loading: false, username: null, error: error.message }));

    function requireLogin() {
      setSession({ loading: false, username: null });
    }
    window.addEventListener("bancosia:auth-required", requireLogin);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("bancosia:auth-required", requireLogin);
  }, []);

  if (session.loading) {
    return <div className="auth-loading">Comprobando sesion...</div>;
  }
  if (!session.username) {
    return (
      <Login
        initialError={session.error}
        onLogin={(username) => setSession({ loading: false, username })}
      />
    );
  }
  return (
    <App
      username={session.username}
      onLogout={async () => {
        try {
          await api.logout();
        } finally {
          setSession({ loading: false, username: null });
        }
      }}
    />
  );
}

function Login({ initialError, onLogin }) {
  const [username, setUsername] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState(initialError || "");
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  async function submit(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setSubmitting(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      const result = await api.login(username, password);
      onLogin(result.username);
    } catch (loginError) {
      setError(loginError.message);
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <main className="login-page">
      <form className="login-card" onSubmit={submit}>
        <div className="login-brand"><LockKeyhole size={28} /></div>
        <h1>Acceso a BancosIA</h1>
        <p className="muted">Introduce tus credenciales para acceder al panel financiero.</p>
        <label>
          Usuario
          <input
            autoComplete="username"
            autoFocus
            value={username}
            onChange={(event) => setUsername(event.target.value)}
            required
          />
        </label>
        <label>
          Contrasena
          <input
            type="password"
            autoComplete="current-password"
            value={password}
            onChange={(event) => setPassword(event.target.value)}
            required
          />
        </label>
        {error && <p className="error login-error">{error}</p>}
        <button className="primary" type="submit" disabled={submitting}>
          {submitting ? "Accediendo..." : "Entrar"}
        </button>
      </form>
    </main>
  );
}

function App({ username, onLogout }) {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState("dashboard");
  const [refreshKey, setRefreshKey] = useState(0);
  const [entities, setEntities] = useState([]);
  const [selectedEntity, setSelectedEntity] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    api.get("/api/entities").then(setEntities).catch(() => setEntities([]));
  }, [refreshKey]);

  function refresh() {
    setRefreshKey((value) => value + 1);
  }

  return (
    <div className="app-shell">
      <aside className="sidebar">
        <div className="brand">
          <Gauge size={24} />
          <div>
            <strong>BancosIA</strong>
            <span>Panel financiero</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <nav>
          {tabs.map((tab) => {
            const Icon = tab.icon;
            return (
              <React.Fragment key={tab.id}>
                <button
                  className={activeTab === tab.id ? "active" : ""}
                  onClick={() => {
                    setActiveTab(tab.id);
                    if (tab.id === "dashboard") refresh();
                  }}
                >
                  <Icon size={18} />
                  {tab.label}
                </button>
                {tab.id === "dashboard" && activeTab === "dashboard" && (
                  <div className="sidebar-subnav">
                    {dashboardSections.map((section) => (
                      <a key={section.id} href={`#${section.id}`}>
                        {section.label}
                      </a>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
              </React.Fragment>
            );
          })}
        </nav>
        <div className="session-panel">
          <span title={username}>{username}</span>
          <button type="button" onClick={onLogout}><LogOut size={16} />Salir</button>
        </div>
      </aside>
      <main>
        <div className="entity-strip">
          <span>Entidad</span>
          <select value={selectedEntity} onChange={(event) => setSelectedEntity(event.target.value)}>
            <option value="">Todas</option>
            {entities.map((entity) => (
              <option key={entity} value={entity}>{entity}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
        {activeTab === "dashboard" && <Dashboard refreshKey={refreshKey} entity={selectedEntity} />}
        {activeTab === "transactions" && <Transactions refreshKey={refreshKey} onRefresh={refresh} entity={selectedEntity} />}
        {activeTab === "rules" && <Rules onRefresh={refresh} entity={selectedEntity} />}
        {activeTab === "budgets" && <Budgets refreshKey={refreshKey} entity={selectedEntity} />}
        {activeTab === "import" && <ImportExport onRefresh={refresh} />}
        {activeTab === "sql" && <SqlBrowser />}
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

function withEntity(path, entity) {
  if (!entity) return path;
  const separator = path.includes("?") ? "&" : "?";
  return `${path}${separator}entity=${encodeURIComponent(entity)}`;
}

function toQueryString(values) {
  const params = new URLSearchParams();
  Object.entries(values).forEach(([key, value]) => {
    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
      value.filter(Boolean).forEach((item) => params.append(key, item));
    } else if (value) {
      params.append(key, value);
    }
  });
  const suffix = params.toString();
  return suffix ? `?${suffix}` : "";
}

--- README ---
# CowsIA

App de registro ganadero creada con una arquitectura similar a BancosIA:

- `backend`: FastAPI, SQLAlchemy y SQLite.
- `frontend`: React, Vite, Recharts y lucide-react.
- Fuente local: `LibroRegistroAnimaleGanaderoAFechas_10-07-2023_10-07-2026.xml`.

## Arranque

Backend:

```powershell
cd C:\ProyectosCodex\Campo\CowsIA\backend
python -m pip install -r requirements.txt
python -m uvicorn app.main:app --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8001
```

Frontend:

```powershell
cd C:\ProyectosCodex\Campo\CowsIA\frontend
npm install
$env:VITE_API_BASE='http://127.0.0.1:8001'
npm run dev -- --port 5174 --strictPort
```

URL local: http://127.0.0.1:5174/

## Verificacion

```powershell
cd C:\ProyectosCodex\Campo\CowsIA\backend
python -m pytest

cd C:\ProyectosCodex\Campo\CowsIA\frontend
npm run build
```

La API importa automaticamente el XML local al arrancar si la base `backend/cows.db` esta vacia.

## Modelo extendido por animal

Cada animal se identifica por `crotal` en la tabla principal `animals`. Los datos multiples se guardan en tablas relacionadas:

- `animal_images`: fotografias del animal, con ruta del fichero y metadatos.
- `animal_locations`: coordenadas del animal, con fecha/hora de registro y origen opcional.
- `animal_events`: sucesos del animal, con texto, fecha, usuario informante e imagen opcional.

La ficha completa se consulta con:

```text
GET /api/animals/{crotal}/details
```

## Carga masiva de imagenes

Las fotos colocadas en `imagenes_animales` se pueden asignar aleatoriamente sin reutilizar imagenes ya marcadas:

```powershell
cd C:\ProyectosCodex\Campo\CowsIA\backend
python -m app.bulk_assign_images ..\imagenes_animales
```

Para simular antes de escribir:

```powershell
python -m app.bulk_assign_images ..\imagenes_animales --dry-run
```

El script guarda una marca por `sha256` en `animal_image_assignments`. Si se suben mas imagenes a la carpeta y se vuelve a ejecutar, solo se asignan las imagenes nuevas; las ya usadas o duplicadas por contenido se omiten.

## Galeria

La pestaña `Galeria` muestra una tarjeta por animal con su imagen principal, crotal, sexo y edad. La API usa:

```text
GET /api/gallery
POST /api/animal-images/{image_id}/primary
```

Cada animal tiene una unica imagen principal. Al migrar datos existentes, queda marcada como principal la primera imagen asignada inicialmente. En la ficha de un animal se puede cambiar manualmente pulsando el boton de imagen sobre cualquiera de sus fotos.

## Carga masiva de ubicaciones

Las coordenadas de `ubicaciones_animales.csv` se asignan aleatoriamente solo a animales activos:

```powershell
cd C:\ProyectosCodex\Campo\CowsIA\backend
python -m app.bulk_assign_locations ..\ubicaciones_animales.csv
```

Para simular antes de escribir:

```powershell
python -m app.bulk_assign_locations ..\ubicaciones_animales.csv --dry-run
```

El script guarda una marca por fila y coordenada en `animal_location_assignments`. Si se vuelve a ejecutar con el mismo CSV, no reasigna las filas ya usadas. Si se añaden nuevas filas al CSV, solo se asignan las nuevas.

## Mapa

La pestaña `Mapa` muestra las ultimas ubicaciones de los animales activos sobre OpenStreetMap. La API usa:

```text
GET /api/map/locations
```

Cada marcador abre un popup con crotal, sexo y edad. Desde el popup se puede abrir la ficha completa del animal.

function formatDate(value) {
  if (!value) return "";
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-ES").format(new Date(`${value}T00:00:00`));
}

function years(value) {
  if (!value) return "";
  const born = new Date(`${value}T00:00:00`);
  const diff = (Date.now() - born.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24 * 365.25);
  return `${diff.toFixed(1)} anos`;
}

function toQueryString(values) {
  const params = new URLSearchParams();
  Object.entries(values).forEach(([key, value]) => {
    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
      value.filter(Boolean).forEach((item) => params.append(key, item));
    } else if (value) {
      params.append(key, value);
    }
  });
  const suffix = params.toString();
  return suffix ? `?${suffix}` : "";
}

function App() {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState("dashboard");
  const [refreshKey, setRefreshKey] = useState(0);

  function refresh() {
    setRefreshKey((value) => value + 1);
  }

  return (
    <div className="app-shell">
      <aside className="sidebar">
        <div className="brand">
          <Gauge size={24} />
          <div>
            <strong>CowsIA</strong>
            <span>Registro ganadero</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <nav>
          {tabs.map((tab) => {
            const Icon = tab.icon;
            return (
              <button key={tab.id} className={activeTab === tab.id ? "active" : ""} onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.id)}>
                <Icon size={18} />
                {tab.label}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </nav>
      </aside>
      <main>
        {activeTab === "dashboard" && <Dashboard refreshKey={refreshKey} />}
        {activeTab === "gallery" && <Gallery refreshKey={refreshKey} />}
        {activeTab === "map" && <CowsMap refreshKey={refreshKey} />}
        {activeTab === "animals" && <Animals refreshKey={refreshKey} />}
        {activeTab === "import" && <ImportXml onRefresh={refresh} />}
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

const animalMarkerIcon = L.divIcon({
  className: "animal-map-marker",
  html: '<span></span>',
  iconSize: [22, 22],
  iconAnchor: [11, 11],
  popupAnchor: [0, -12],
});

function CowsMap({ refreshKey }) {
  const [rows, setRows] = useState([]);
  const [selectedAnimal, setSelectedAnimal] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    api.get("/api/map/locations").then(setRows);
  }, [refreshKey]);

  const center = useMemo(() => {
    if (rows.length === 0) return [39.96, -6.48];
    const lat = rows.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.latitude, 0) / rows.length;
    const lng = rows.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.longitude, 0) / rows.length;
    return [lat, lng];
  }, [rows]);

  return (
    <section className="page map-page">
      <PageTitle title="Mapa" />
      <div className="map-summary">
        <span>{rows.length} animales con ubicacion</span>
      </div>
      <div className="map-panel">
        <MapContainer key={`${center[0]}-${center[1]}-${rows.length}`} center={center} zoom={15} scrollWheelZoom className="animal-map">
          <TileLayer
            attribution='&copy; <a href="https://www.openstreetmap.org/copyright">OpenStreetMap</a>'
            url="https://{s}.tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png"
          />
          {rows.map((row) => (
            <Marker key={`${row.animal_id}-${row.latitude}-${row.longitude}`} position={[row.latitude, row.longitude]} icon={animalMarkerIcon}>
              <Popup>
                <div className="map-popup">
                  {row.image_url && <img src={api.url(row.image_url)} alt={row.crotal} />}
                  <strong>{row.crotal}</strong>
                  <span>{row.sexo || "Sin sexo"} · {row.age_years === null ? "Sin edad" : `${row.age_years} anos`}</span>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() =>
                      setSelectedAnimal({
                        crotal: row.crotal,
                        sexo: row.sexo,
                        fecha_nacimiento: row.fecha_nacimiento,
                      })
                    }
                  >
                    Abrir ficha
                  </button>
                </div>
              </Popup>
            </Marker>
          ))}
        </MapContainer>
      </div>
      {selectedAnimal && <AnimalDetail animal={selectedAnimal} onClose={() => setSelectedAnimal(null)} onChanged={() => api.get("/api/map/locations").then(setRows)} />}
    </section>
  );
}

function PiePanel({ title, rows }) {
  return (
    <ChartPanel title={title}>
      <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={250}>
        <PieChart>
          <Pie data={rows} dataKey="value" nameKey="name" outerRadius={86}>
            {rows.map((_, index) => (
              <Cell key={index} fill={palette[index % palette.length]} />
            ))}
          </Pie>
          <Tooltip />
        </PieChart>
      </ResponsiveContainer>
    </ChartPanel>
  );
}

function ListPanel({ title, rows }) {
  return (
    <div className="panel list-panel">
      <h2>{title}</h2>
      {rows.length === 0 && <p className="muted">Sin datos</p>}
      {rows.map((row) => (
        <div className="list-row" key={row.name}>
          <span>{row.name}</span>
          <strong>{row.value}</strong>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

function PageTitle({ title, action }) {
  return (
    <header className="page-title">
      <h1>{title}</h1>
      {action}
    </header>
  );
}

function Metric({ label, value, tone }) {
  return (
    <div className={`metric ${tone || ""}`}>
      <span>{label}</span>
      <strong>{value}</strong>
    </div>
  );
}

function ChartPanel({ title, children }) {
  return (
    <div className="panel">
      <h2>{title}</h2>
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

--- Bancos client ---
const API_BASE = import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE ?? "";

async function request(path, options = {}) {
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${path}`, { credentials: "include", ...options });
  if (!response.ok) {
    const error = await response.json().catch(() => ({ detail: response.statusText }));
    if (response.status === 401 && !path.startsWith("/api/auth/")) {
      window.dispatchEvent(new Event("bancosia:auth-required"));
    }
    throw new Error(error.detail || "Error de API");
  }
  return response.json();
}

export const api = {
  get: (path) => request(path),
  post: (path, body) =>
    request(path, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify(body),
    }),
  patch: (path, body) =>
    request(path, {
      method: "PATCH",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify(body),
    }),
  delete: (path) => request(path, { method: "DELETE" }),
  upload: (path, file) => {
    const form = new FormData();
    form.append("file", file);
    return request(path, { method: "POST", body: form });
  },
  login: (username, password) =>
    request("/api/auth/login", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ username, password }),
    }),
  logout: () => request("/api/auth/logout", { method: "POST" }),
  session: () => request("/api/auth/session"),
  url: (path) => `${API_BASE}${path}`,
};

2. CAMBIOS REALIZADOS
---------------------

Se utilizo apply_patch para realizar todos los cambios de archivos.

Archivos creados:

  backend/app/auth.py
    - Lectura y validacion de BANCOSIA_USERNAME, BANCOSIA_PASSWORD,
      BANCOSIA_SESSION_SECRET, BANCOSIA_SESSION_HOURS y
      BANCOSIA_COOKIE_SECURE.
    - Comparacion de credenciales con hmac.compare_digest.

    - Exclusion del archivo .env.

  backend/app/main.py
    - Middleware que protege todas las rutas /api salvo salud y autenticacion.
    - POST /api/auth/login.

    - POST /api/auth/logout.

+### Docker Compose
+
+La configuracion Docker publica CowsIA en el puerto 8081, por lo que puede
+ejecutarse al mismo tiempo que BancosIA_2 (puerto 8080):
+
+```bash
+docker compose up --build -d
+```
+
+Abre http://localhost:8081 e inicia sesion con las credenciales guardadas en
+`.env`. Para cambiarlas, edita `COWSIA_USERNAME` y `COWSIA_PASSWORD` antes
+de arrancar los contenedores. El backend no publica ningun puerto en el host:
+solo Nginx puede acceder a el dentro de la red privada de Compose.
+
+Para detener CowsIA:
+
+```bash
+docker compose down
+```
+
+La base SQLite, las imagenes subidas y el XML local se montan desde el
+directorio del proyecto, por lo que no se pierden al recrear los contenedores.

Add: /opt/DesplieguesDocker/CowsIA/backend/app/auth.py
import base64
import hashlib
import hmac
import json
import os
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass

from fastapi import Request


SESSION_COOKIE_NAME = "cowsia_session"
DEFAULT_SESSION_HOURS = 12


class AuthConfigurationError(RuntimeError):
    pass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AuthSettings:
    username: str
    password: str
    session_secret: str
    session_seconds: int
    cookie_secure: bool


def get_auth_settings() -> AuthSettings:
    username = os.getenv("COWSIA_USERNAME", "").strip()
    password = os.getenv("COWSIA_PASSWORD", "")
    session_secret = os.getenv("COWSIA_SESSION_SECRET", "")
    if not username or not password:
        raise AuthConfigurationError("COWSIA_USERNAME y COWSIA_PASSWORD son obligatorios")
    if len(session_secret) < 32:
        raise AuthConfigurationError("COWSIA_SESSION_SECRET debe tener al menos 32 caracteres")

    try:
        session_hours = int(os.getenv("COWSIA_SESSION_HOURS", str(DEFAULT_SESSION_HOURS)))
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise AuthConfigurationError("COWSIA_SESSION_HOURS debe ser un numero entero") from exc
    if session_hours < 1:
        raise AuthConfigurationError("COWSIA_SESSION_HOURS debe ser mayor que cero")

    cookie_secure = os.getenv("COWSIA_COOKIE_SECURE", "false").strip().lower() in {"1", "true", "yes", "on"}
    return AuthSettings(username, password, session_secret, session_hours * 3600, cookie_secure)


def credentials_are_valid(username: str, password: str, settings: AuthSettings) -> bool:
    return hmac.compare_digest(username.encode(), settings.username.encode()) and hmac.compare_digest(
        password.encode(), settings.password.encode()
    )


def create_session_token(username: str, settings: AuthSettings, now: int | None = None) -> str:
    payload = {
        "sub": username,
        "exp": (now if now is not None else int(time.time())) + settings.session_seconds,
    }
    encoded_payload = _encode(json.dumps(payload, separators=(",", ":")).encode())
    signature = hmac.new(settings.session_secret.encode(), encoded_payload.encode(), hashlib.sha256).digest()
    return f"{encoded_payload}.{_encode(signature)}"


def session_username(token: str | None, settings: AuthSettings, now: int | None = None) -> str | None:
    if not token:
        return None
    try:
        encoded_payload, encoded_signature = token.split(".", 1)
        expected_signature = hmac.new(
            settings.session_secret.encode(), encoded_payload.encode(), hashlib.sha256
        ).digest()
        if not hmac.compare_digest(_decode(encoded_signature), expected_signature):
            return None
        payload = json.loads(_decode(encoded_payload))
        current_time = now if now is not None else int(time.time())
        if payload.get("sub") != settings.username or int(payload.get("exp", 0)) <= current_time:
            return None
        return settings.username
    except (ValueError, TypeError, json.JSONDecodeError):
        return None


def authenticated_username(request: Request, settings: AuthSettings) -> str | None:
    return session_username(request.cookies.get(SESSION_COOKIE_NAME), settings)


def _encode(value: bytes) -> str:
    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(value).rstrip(b"=").decode()


def _decode(value: str) -> bytes:
    return base64.urlsafe_b64decode(value + "=" * (-len(value) % 4))

+PUBLIC_API_PATHS = {"/api/health", "/api/auth/login", "/api/auth/session", "/api/auth/logout"}
+

+@app.middleware("http")
+async def require_authentication(request: Request, call_next):
+    if request.method == "OPTIONS" or not request.url.path.startswith("/api/") or request.url.path in PUBLIC_API_PATHS:
+        return await call_next(request)
+    try:
+        settings = get_auth_settings()
+    except AuthConfigurationError as exc:
+        return JSONResponse(status_code=503, content={"detail": str(exc)})
+    if not authenticated_username(request, settings):
+        return JSONResponse(status_code=401, content={"detail": "Debes iniciar sesion"})
+    return await call_next(request)

@@ -52,2 +75,40 @@
 
+@app.post("/api/auth/login")
+def login(payload: LoginIn, response: Response) -> dict[str, str | bool]:
+    try:
+        settings = get_auth_settings()
+    except AuthConfigurationError as exc:
+        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail=str(exc)) from exc
+    if not credentials_are_valid(payload.username, payload.password, settings):
+        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Usuario o contrasena incorrectos")
+    response.set_cookie(
+        key=SESSION_COOKIE_NAME,
+        value=create_session_token(settings.username, settings),
+        max_age=settings.session_seconds,
+        httponly=True,
+        secure=settings.cookie_secure,
+        samesite="strict",
+        path="/api",
+    )
+    response.headers["Cache-Control"] = "no-store"
+    return {"authenticated": True, "username": settings.username}
+
+
+@app.get("/api/auth/session")
+def auth_session(request: Request) -> dict[str, str | bool | None]:
+    try:
+        settings = get_auth_settings()
+    except AuthConfigurationError as exc:
+        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail=str(exc)) from exc
+    username = authenticated_username(request, settings)
+    return {"authenticated": bool(username), "username": username}
+
+
+@app.post("/api/auth/logout")
+def logout(response: Response) -> dict[str, bool]:
+    response.delete_cookie(SESSION_COOKIE_NAME, path="/api")
+    response.headers["Cache-Control"] = "no-store"
+    return {"authenticated": False}
+
+
 @app.get("/api/local-xml/status")

Add: /opt/DesplieguesDocker/CowsIA/backend/tests/test_auth.py
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app.auth import create_session_token, get_auth_settings, session_username
from app.main import app


def configure_auth(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("COWSIA_USERNAME", "usuario-prueba")
    monkeypatch.setenv("COWSIA_PASSWORD", "contrasena-prueba")
    monkeypatch.setenv("COWSIA_SESSION_SECRET", "secreto-de-prueba-con-mas-de-32-caracteres")


def test_protected_api_requires_login(monkeypatch):
    configure_auth(monkeypatch)
    client = TestClient(app)

    response = client.get("/api/animals")

    assert response.status_code == 401


def test_login_creates_session_and_logout_removes_it(monkeypatch):
    configure_auth(monkeypatch)
    client = TestClient(app)

    bad_login = client.post("/api/auth/login", json={"username": "usuario-prueba", "password": "incorrecta"})
    assert bad_login.status_code == 401

    login = client.post(
        "/api/auth/login",
        json={"username": "usuario-prueba", "password": "contrasena-prueba"},
    )
    assert login.status_code == 200
    assert client.get("/api/auth/session").json() == {
        "authenticated": True,
        "username": "usuario-prueba",
    }

    logout = client.post("/api/auth/logout")
    assert logout.status_code == 200
    assert client.get("/api/auth/session").json()["authenticated"] is False


def test_expired_and_modified_tokens_are_rejected(monkeypatch):
    configure_auth(monkeypatch)
    settings = get_auth_settings()
    token = create_session_token(settings.username, settings, now=100)

    assert session_username(token, settings, now=101) == settings.username
    assert session_username(token, settings, now=100 + settings.session_seconds) is None
    assert session_username(f"{token}modificado", settings, now=101) is None

+      .catch((error) => setSession({ loading: false, username: null, error: error.message }));
+
+    function requireLogin() {
+      setSession({ loading: false, username: null });
+    }
+    window.addEventListener("cowsia:auth-required", requireLogin);
+    return () => window.removeEventListener("cowsia:auth-required", requireLogin);
+  }, []);
+
+  if (session.loading) {
+    return <div className="auth-loading">Comprobando sesion...</div>;
+  }
+  if (!session.username) {
+    return <Login initialError={session.error} onLogin={(username) => setSession({ loading: false, username })} />;
+  }
+  return (
+    <App
+      username={session.username}
+      onLogout={async () => {
+        try {
+          await api.logout();
+        } finally {
+          setSession({ loading: false, username: null });
+        }
+      }}
+    />
+  );
+}
+
+function Login({ initialError, onLogin }) {
+  const [username, setUsername] = useState("");
+  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
+  const [error, setError] = useState(initialError || "");
+  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
+
+  async function submit(event) {
+    event.preventDefault();
+    setSubmitting(true);
+    setError("");
+    try {
+      const result = await api.login(username, password);
+      onLogin(result.username);
+    } catch (loginError) {
+      setError(loginError.message);
+    } finally {
+      setSubmitting(false);
+    }
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <main className="login-page">
+      <form className="login-card" onSubmit={submit}>
+        <div className="login-brand"><LockKeyhole size={28} /></div>
+        <h1>Acceso a CowsIA</h1>
+        <p className="muted">Introduce tus credenciales para acceder al registro ganadero.</p>
+        <label>
+          Usuario
+          <input
+            autoComplete="username"
+            autoFocus
+            value={username}
+            onChange={(event) => setUsername(event.target.value)}
+            required
+          />
+        </label>
+        <label>
+          Contrasena
+          <input
+            type="password"
+            autoComplete="current-password"
+            value={password}
+            onChange={(event) => setPassword(event.target.value)}
+            required
+          />
+        </label>
+        {error && <p className="error login-error">{error}</p>}
+        <button className="primary" type="submit" disabled={submitting}>
+          {submitting ? "Accediendo..." : "Entrar"}
+        </button>
+      </form>
+    </main>
+  );
+}

Assistant

La configuración de Compose es válida. Las pruebas locales no pueden ejecutarse directamente porque este traslado conserva entornos virtuales y node_modules de otra plataforma (vite no es ejecutable aquí y el Python del sistema no tiene pytest). Esto no afecta a las imágenes Docker, que instalan dependencias limpias; usaré precisamente la construcción Docker para validar ambas partes y después probaré el acceso protegido por HTTP.

@app.get("/api/animal-images/{image_id}/file")
def get_animal_image(image_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> FileResponse:
    row = db.get(AnimalImage, image_id)
    if not row:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Imagen no encontrada")
    path = Path(row.file_path)
    if not path.exists():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Imagen no encontrada en disco")
    return FileResponse(path, filename=row.file_name or path.name, media_type=row.content_type)


@app.delete("/api/animal-images/{image_id}")
def delete_animal_image(image_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict[str, bool]:
    row = db.get(AnimalImage, image_id)
    if not row:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Imagen no encontrada")
    path = Path(row.file_path)
    if path.exists():
        path.unlink()
    animal_id = row.animal_id
    was_primary = bool(row.is_primary)
    db.delete(row)
    db.flush()
    if was_primary:
        next_image = (
            db.query(AnimalImage)
            .filter(AnimalImage.animal_id == animal_id)
            .order_by(AnimalImage.created_at.asc(), AnimalImage.id.asc())
            .first()
        )
        if next_image:
            next_image.is_primary = True
    db.commit()
    return {"ok": True}


@app.post("/api/animals/{crotal}/locations", response_model=AnimalLocationOut)
def create_animal_location(crotal: str, payload: AnimalLocationIn, db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> AnimalLocation:
    animal = _animal_by_crotal(db, crotal)
    row = AnimalLocation(
        animal_id=animal.id,
        latitude=payload.latitude,
        longitude=payload.longitude,
        recorded_at=payload.recorded_at or datetime.utcnow(),
        source=payload.source,
    )
    db.add(row)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(row)
    return row

@app.post("/api/animals/{crotal}/events", response_model=AnimalEventOut)
def create_animal_event(crotal: str, payload: AnimalEventIn, db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict:
    animal = _animal_by_crotal(db, crotal)
    text = payload.text.strip()
    if not text:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="El texto del suceso no puede estar vacio")
    row = AnimalEvent(animal_id=animal.id, text=text, event_date=payload.event_date, reporter=payload.reporter)
    db.add(row)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(row)
    return _event_out(row)

@app.get("/api/animals/{crotal}/details", response_model=AnimalDetailOut)
def animal_details(crotal: str, db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict:
    animal = _animal_by_crotal(db, crotal)
    images = db.query(AnimalImage).filter(AnimalImage.animal_id == animal.id).order_by(AnimalImage.is_primary.desc(), AnimalImage.created_at.asc(), AnimalImage.id.asc()).all()
    locations = db.query(AnimalLocation).filter(AnimalLocation.animal_id == animal.id).order_by(AnimalLocation.recorded_at.desc()).all()
    events = db.query(AnimalEvent).filter(AnimalEvent.animal_id == animal.id).order_by(AnimalEvent.event_date.desc().nullslast(), AnimalEvent.created_at.desc()).all()
    return {
        "animal": animal,
        "images": [_image_out(row) for row in images],
        "locations": locations,
        "events": [_event_out(row) for row in events],
    }


@app.post("/api/animals/{crotal}/images", response_model=AnimalImageOut)
async def create_animal_image(crotal: str, file: UploadFile = File(...), db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict:
    animal = _animal_by_crotal(db, crotal)
    suffix = _image_suffix(file)
    content = await file.read()
    target = _store_upload("animal_images", file, content, suffix)
    row = AnimalImage(
        animal_id=animal.id,
        file_path=str(target),
        file_name=file.filename or target.name,
        content_type=file.content_type,
        is_primary=db.query(AnimalImage).filter(AnimalImage.animal_id == animal.id, AnimalImage.is_primary.is_(True)).first() is None,
    )
    db.add(row)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(row)
    return _image_out(row)


@app.get("/api/gallery", response_model=list[GalleryAnimalOut])
def gallery(db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> list[dict]:
    animals = db.query(Animal).order_by(Animal.crotal.asc()).all()
    rows = []
    for animal in animals:
        image = (
            db.query(AnimalImage)
            .filter(AnimalImage.animal_id == animal.id)
            .order_by(AnimalImage.is_primary.desc(), AnimalImage.created_at.asc(), AnimalImage.id.asc())
            .first()
        )
    crotal: str,
    text: str = Form(...),
    event_date: date | None = Form(None),
    reporter: str | None = Form(None),
    file: UploadFile | None = File(None),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
) -> dict:
    animal = _animal_by_crotal(db, crotal)
    clean_text = text.strip()
    if not clean_text:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="El texto del suceso no puede estar vacio")
    image_path = None
    image_name = None
    image_content_type = None
    if file and file.filename:
        suffix = _image_suffix(file)
        content = await file.read()
        target = _store_upload("event_images", file, content, suffix)
        image_path = str(target)
        image_name = file.filename or target.name
        image_content_type = file.content_type
    row = AnimalEvent(
        animal_id=animal.id,
        text=clean_text,
        event_date=event_date,
        reporter=reporter,
        image_path=image_path,
        image_name=image_name,
        image_content_type=image_content_type,
    )
    db.add(row)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(row)
    return _event_out(row)


@app.get("/api/animal-events/{event_id}/image")
def get_animal_event_image(event_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> FileResponse:
    row = db.get(AnimalEvent, event_id)
    if not row or not row.image_path:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Imagen no encontrada")
    path = Path(row.image_path)
    if not path.exists():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Imagen no encontrada en disco")
    return FileResponse(path, filename=row.image_name or path.name, media_type=row.image_content_type)


@app.delete("/api/animal-events/{event_id}")
def delete_animal_event(event_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict[str, bool]:
    row = db.get(AnimalEvent, event_id)
    if not row:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Suceso no encontrado")
    if row.image_path:
        path = Path(row.image_path)
        if path.exists():
            path.unlink()
    db.delete(row)
    db.commit()
    return {"ok": True}


@app.get("/api/filter-options")
def filter_options(db: Session = Depends(get_db)) -> dict:
    def values(column) -> list[str]:
        return [row[0] for row in db.query(column).distinct().order_by(column.asc()).all() if row[0]]

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import mimetypes
import random
import shutil
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from uuid import uuid4

from sqlalchemy import func

from app.database import SessionLocal, init_db
from app.models import Animal, AnimalImage, AnimalImageAssignment

WORKSPACE_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
DEFAULT_SOURCE_DIR = WORKSPACE_ROOT / "imagenes_animales"
UPLOAD_DIR = WORKSPACE_ROOT / "backend" / "uploads" / "animal_images"
IMAGE_EXTENSIONS = {".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".webp", ".gif"}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SourceImage:
    path: Path
    sha256: str
    size: int


def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def discover_images(source_dir: Path) -> list[Path]:
    if not source_dir.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"No existe la carpeta de imagenes: {source_dir}")
    return sorted(
        path
        for path in source_dir.rglob("*")
        if path.is_file() and path.suffix.lower() in IMAGE_EXTENSIONS
    )


def load_new_source_images(source_dir: Path, used_hashes: set[str]) -> tuple[list[SourceImage], int]:
    images: list[SourceImage] = []
    skipped_used = 0
    seen_hashes = set(used_hashes)
    for path in discover_images(source_dir):
        fingerprint = sha256_file(path)
        if fingerprint in seen_hashes:
            skipped_used += 1
            continue
        seen_hashes.add(fingerprint)
        images.append(SourceImage(path=path.resolve(), sha256=fingerprint, size=path.stat().st_size))
    return images, skipped_used


def cleanup_orphan_bulk_files(db) -> int:
    if not UPLOAD_DIR.exists():
        return 0
    referenced = {
        row[0]
        for row in db.query(AnimalImage.file_path).filter(AnimalImage.file_path.like("%bulk_%")).all()
    }
    removed = 0
    for path in UPLOAD_DIR.glob("bulk_*"):
        if str(path) not in referenced:
            path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
            removed += 1
    return removed


def choose_animal_id(counts: dict[int, int], rng: random.Random) -> int:
    min_count = min(counts.values())
    candidates = [animal_id for animal_id, count in counts.items() if count == min_count]
    return rng.choice(candidates)


def destination_for(source: SourceImage) -> Path:
    suffix = source.path.suffix.lower()
    return UPLOAD_DIR / f"bulk_{uuid4().hex}{suffix}"


def content_type_for(path: Path) -> str:
    return mimetypes.guess_type(path.name)[0] or "application/octet-stream"


def assign_images(source_dir: Path, dry_run: bool = False, seed: int | None = None) -> dict:
    init_db()
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        animals = db.query(Animal).order_by(Animal.id.asc()).all()
        if not animals:
            raise RuntimeError("No hay animales cargados en la base de datos")

        orphan_files_removed = 0 if dry_run else cleanup_orphan_bulk_files(db)

        used_hashes = {
            row[0]
            for row in db.query(AnimalImageAssignment.source_sha256).all()
        }
        source_images, skipped_used = load_new_source_images(source_dir, used_hashes)
        rng.shuffle(source_images)

        existing_counts = dict(
            db.query(AnimalImage.animal_id, func.count(AnimalImage.id))
            .group_by(AnimalImage.animal_id)
            .all()
        )
        existing_primary = {
            row[0]
            for row in db.query(AnimalImage.animal_id)
            .filter(AnimalImage.is_primary.is_(True))
            .distinct()
            .all()
        }
        counts = {animal.id: int(existing_counts.get(animal.id, 0)) for animal in animals}
        has_primary = {animal.id: animal.id in existing_primary for animal in animals}
        animal_by_id = {animal.id: animal for animal in animals}

        assignments: list[tuple[SourceImage, int]] = []
        for source in source_images:
            animal_id = choose_animal_id(counts, rng)
            assignments.append((source, animal_id))
            counts[animal_id] += 1

        if dry_run:
            without_photo = sum(1 for count in counts.values() if count == 0)
            return {
                "dry_run": True,
                "source_dir": str(source_dir),
                "available_images": len(source_images),
                "skipped_used_images": skipped_used,
                "orphan_files_removed": orphan_files_removed,
                "planned_assignments": len(assignments),
                "animals": len(animals),
                "animals_without_photo_after": without_photo,
                "min_photos_per_animal_after": min(counts.values()),
                "max_photos_per_animal_after": max(counts.values()),
            }

        UPLOAD_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        inserted = 0
        for source, animal_id in assignments:
            target = destination_for(source)
            shutil.copy2(source.path, target)
            image = AnimalImage(
                animal_id=animal_id,
                file_path=str(target),
                file_name=source.path.name,
                content_type=content_type_for(source.path),
                is_primary=not has_primary[animal_id],
            )
            has_primary[animal_id] = True
            db.add(image)
            db.flush()
            db.add(
                AnimalImageAssignment(
                    animal_image_id=image.id,
                    animal_id=animal_id,
                    source_path=str(source.path),
                    source_sha256=source.sha256,
                    source_size=source.size,
                    assigned_by_bulk=True,
                )
            )
            inserted += 1

        db.commit()
        final_counts = counts.values()
        return {
            "dry_run": False,
            "source_dir": str(source_dir),
            "assigned_images": inserted,
            "skipped_used_images": skipped_used,
            "orphan_files_removed": orphan_files_removed,
            "animals": len(animals),
            "animals_without_photo_after": sum(1 for count in final_counts if count == 0),
            "min_photos_per_animal_after": min(final_counts),
            "max_photos_per_animal_after": max(final_counts),
            "sample": [
                {
                    "image": source.path.name,
                    "crotal": animal_by_id[animal_id].crotal,
                }
✓ • 0ms

+def _portable_upload_path(path: Path) -> str:

+def _resolve_upload_path(stored_path: str, folder: str) -> Path:
+    path = Path(stored_path)
+    candidates = [path]
+    if not path.is_absolute():
+        candidates.append(WORKSPACE_ROOT / path)
+    stored_name = PureWindowsPath(stored_path).name
+    if stored_name:
+        candidates.append(UPLOAD_ROOT / folder / stored_name)
+    return next((candidate for candidate in candidates if candidate.is_file()), candidates[-1])
